## Embedded Files
2179c9ab26ade382d0216564a49c17d96b898514: $$\begin{cases}
x_1 = f_1(u_1,\dots,u_n) \\
x_2 = f_2(u_1,\dots,u_n) \\
\dots \\
x_n = f_n(u_1,\dots,u_n)
\end{cases}$$

cb0fc20a088ca0065ed384dc91cb2127918ba683: $$\underbrace{\int \dots \int}_{D} f(x_1,\dots,x_n) \, dx_1 \dots dx_n = \underbrace{\int \dots \int}_{D} f(u_1,\dots,u_n) |\det(J)| \, du_1 \dots du_n$$

f44a616a13798dd6391d0080ad5535a35122a497: $$\begin{bmatrix}
\cos \theta & -\rho \sin \theta \\
\sin \theta & \rho \cos \theta
\end{bmatrix}
\implies |\det(J)| = \rho$$

d425bef7d694784bdc67cfe2fd5d00f5d9f68a84: $$\int \int_D dxdy =$$

302109b099685bdf57c2c1f6933e584c90e07b00: $$\int \int_D f(x,y) \, \, dxdy = \sum_{i=1}^2 \int \int_{D_i} f(x,y) \, \, dxdy$$

d293fbd7b4fd1c83c9d6ac66bee36fecd57d0fc3: $$\int \int_D f(x,y) \, \, dxdy$$

000783ec378e78e506274c6cdd157f9684f4e8ec: $$\int_c^d \left( \int_a^b f(x,y) \, \, dx \right) dy = \int_a^b \left( \int_c^d f(x,y) \, \, dy \right) dx$$
